Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?time) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?time) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?p, ?f) ^ onto:NotifyNurse(?n) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?r) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 Class: onto:NotifyDoctor

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:comment "Notify doctor for event (alert notification)",
        rdfs:label "Notify Doctor"
    
    SubClassOf: 
        onto:AlertingNotification

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?obs) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?p, ?f) ^ onto:NotifyNurse(?n) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?r) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?f) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?p, ?f) ^ onto:NotifyNurse(?n) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?r) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 Class: <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>

    Annotations:

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?time) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Annotations:

    terms:license "CC0",
    rdfs:comment "The designed Wear4PDmoveOnto aims to semantically integrate heterogeneous data such as dynamic/stream data  (collected from wearables) and static/historic data (stored in health databases), in order to represent personal health knowledge in the form of a KG, supporting health apps’ reasoning capabilities for high-level events recognition (e.g., ‘missing dose’ or  ‘patient fall’ event).",
    rdfs:label "Wear4PDmoveOnto"@en,
    owl:versionInfo "v1.0.0"

AnnotationProperty: core:hasTimestamp

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:label "dose time"

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?time) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

    rdfs:comment "The designed Wear4PDmoveOnto aims to semantically integrate heterogeneous data such as dynamic/stream data  (collected from wearables) and static/historic data (stored in health databases), in order to represent personal health knowledge in the form of a KG, supporting health apps’ reasoning capabilities for high-level events recognition (e.g., ‘missing dose’ or  ‘patient fall’ event).",

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:ParkinsonDiseasePatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ onto:DosageTime(?doseTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseTime, ?doseDateTime) ^ swrlb:addDayTimeDurationToDateTime(?expectedTime, ?doseDateTime, "PT30M"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ swrlb:equal(?obsTime, ?expectedTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?notification) -> onto:sendNotification(?notification, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.

SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ sosa:Observation(?o) ^ onto:obsAfterDosing(?o, true) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?o)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.

SWRL Rule:
 schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:isMonitored(?p, true) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?m) ^ onto:hasMedicalPrescription(?p, ?m) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?m, "Dopamine") -> onto:PDPatient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) ^ onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?e), 2) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)

    Domain: 
        onto:Patient

    Annotations: 
        rdfs:label "is Performed By"
    
    InverseOf: 
        onto:performs
		
Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseStart) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseEnd) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseStart, ?startTime) ^ core:hasTimestamp(?doseEnd, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?endTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ sosa:Observation(?tObs) ^ sosa:Observation(?bObs) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?tremor) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?brady) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?tObs, ?p) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?bObs, ?p) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?tObs, ?tremor) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?bObs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?tObs, ?tTime) ^ sosa:resultTime(?bObs, ?bTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tTime, ?endTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?startTime) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bTime, ?endTime) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?n, true)
